title: Sign Library
description: Every standard sign, drawn clean at the same size, grouped by MUTCD type, with California status, sizes, and governing sections.
tableOfContents: false
---

<div class="srcbar">Artwork and dimensions: FHWA Standard Highway Signs, 2024 Edition (Releases 1-4). California status: CA MUTCD 2026 figures. 735 signs; 28 are crossed out (not adopted) in California.</div>

<div class="sseries" data-cat="ttc"><h3 id="series-w23">W23 &middot; Traffic pattern</h3><div class="sgrid"><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w23-1/" data-code="W23-1" data-name="slow traffic ahead work zone sign w23-1 slow traffic ahead work zone sign traffic pattern black orange" data-cat="ttc" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W23-1.svg" alt="W23-1 Slow Traffic Ahead"></span><span class="scode">W23-1</span><span class="sname">Slow Traffic Ahead work zone sign</span></a></div></div>
<div class="sseries" data-cat="ttc"><h3 id="series-w24">W24 &middot; Shifts</h3><div class="sgrid"><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w24-1/" data-code="W24-1" data-name="double reverse curve (1 lane) work zone sign w24-1 double reverse curve (1 lane) work zone sign shifts black orange" data-cat="ttc" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W24-1.svg" alt="W24-1 Double Reverse Curve (1 lane)"></span><span class="scode">W24-1</span><span class="sname">Double Reverse Curve (1 lane) work zone sign</span></a><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w24-1a/" data-code="W24-1a" data-name="double reverse curve (2 lanes) work zone sign w24-1a double reverse curve (2 lanes) work zone sign shifts black orange" data-cat="ttc" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W24-1a.svg" alt="W24-1a Double Reverse Curve (2 lanes)"></span><span class="scode">W24-1a</span><span class="sname">Double Reverse Curve (2 lanes) work zone sign</span></a><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w24-1b/" data-code="W24-1b" data-name="double reverse curve (3 lanes) work zone sign w24-1b double reverse curve (3 lanes) work zone sign shifts black orange" data-cat="ttc" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W24-1b.svg" alt="W24-1b Double Reverse Curve (3 lanes)"></span><span class="scode">W24-1b</span><span class="sname">Double Reverse Curve (3 lanes) work zone sign</span></a><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/w24-1cp/" data-code="W24-1cP" data-name="all lanes plaque w24-1cp all lanes (plaque) work zone sign shifts black orange" data-cat="ttc" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/W24-1cP.svg" alt="W24-1cP All Lanes (plaque)"></span><span class="scode">W24-1cP</span><span class="sname">All Lanes plaque</span></a></div></div>

<script>
(() => {
  const q = document.getElementById('sq'), ca = document.getElementById('sca'), cnt = document.getElementById('scount');
  let cat = 'all';
  const cards = [...document.querySelectorAll('.scard')];
  function run() {
    const t = q.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    let n = 0;
    cards.forEach(c => {
      const ok = (cat === 'all' || c.dataset.cat === cat) && (!ca.checked || c.dataset.ca !== 'not-adopted') &&
        (!t || c.dataset.code.toLowerCase().includes(t) || c.dataset.name.includes(t));
      c.hidden = !ok; if (ok) n++;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.sseries').forEach(s => s.hidden = !s.querySelector('.scard:not([hidden])'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.sgroup').forEach(h => {
      let el = h.nextElementSibling, any = false;
      while (el && !el.classList.contains('sgroup')) { if (el.classList.contains('sseries') && !el.hidden) any = true; el = el.nextElementSibling; }
      h.hidden = !any;
    });
    cnt.textContent = n + ' signs';
  }
  q.addEventListener('input', run); ca.addEventListener('change', run);
  document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on')); b.classList.add('on'); cat = b.dataset.f; run();
  }));
  const h = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('q'); if (h) q.value = h;
  run();
})();
</script>
